# 全项目文件地图（V2 基线）

> 生成时间由本地 Git 已跟踪文件决定。每一行都是一个文件，不以目录概览替代文件覆盖。此地图只证明**纳入教学计划**；正式课程完成后，还需在 `精读`、`运行`、`练习`、`验收` 四列逐项打勾。

## 范围与规则

- 基线：`git ls-files` 返回的 2257 个文件。
- `source`：必须有直接精读卡，不能被概览课替代。
- `test-source`：必须与生产文件配对阅读，解释它证明的行为与未证明的风险。
- `configuration`、`template-or-skill`、`documentation`：必须进入相应课程或参考课，并注明用途。
- `binary-or-static-asset`：记录消费者和用途；不把图片/二进制伪装成源码精读。
- `learning-material`：现有学习笔记本身仅索引，不计入项目源码覆盖率。

## 类型统计

| 类型 | 文件数 | 默认教学动作 |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| automation-script | 2 | Script reading: explain input, side effects, failure handling, and when the script is run. |
| binary-or-static-asset | 48 | Asset index: identify consumer and purpose; do not pretend binary internals are source lessons. |
| configuration | 43 | Configuration reading: explain each active field and the runtime/build consequence. |
| documentation | 703 | Reference reading: classify as normative, design history, API guide, QA evidence, or archive. |
| learning-material | 155 | Curriculum artifact: not project implementation; index only to avoid circular teaching claims. |
| markup-source | 8 | Markup reading: explain DOM structure, script/style entry points, and runtime role. |
| runtime-data | 71 | Data reading: explain producer, schema, consumer, lifecycle, and whether it is a fixture or local state. |
| source | 892 | Direct reading: explain every exported symbol, call site, branch, and failure path. |
| style-source | 3 | Style reading: explain selector scope, design token, responsive rule, and the component it affects. |
| template-or-skill | 178 | Template reading: explain variable contract, lifecycle, and generated output boundary. |
| test-source | 154 | Test reading: pair with production source; explain fixture, assertion, and uncovered risk. |
